- **whatchanged**: Walk reachable commits and pair each with the raw changes it introduced — git's `git whatchanged` (the modern alias for `git log --raw --no-merges`). Returns **structured data only**: each entry reuses [`log`](log.md)'s commit projection plus a `TreeDiff` of the changes against the first parent (root: against the empty tree). Merge commits are excluded from the output. The `--raw` line rendering ( ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/whatchanged.md) · ★4)
